\\n440: \\t\\t\\ts.logger.Debug(\\\"segment based matched\\\", zap.Int(\\\"rank\\\", int(rollout.Rank)), zap.Strings(\\\"segments\\\", segmentKeys))\\n441: \\t\\t\\treturn resp, nil\\n442: \\t\\t}\\n443: \\t}\\n444: \\n445: \\t// If we have exhausted all rollouts and we still don't have a match, return flag enabled value.\\n446: \\tresp.Reason = zap.Bool(\\\"enabled\\\", flag.Enabled))\\n451: \\treturn resp, nil\\n452: }\\n453: \\n454: // Batch takes in a list of *evaluation.EvaluationRequest and returns their respective responses.\\n455: int(d.Rollout*percentMultiplier)\n241: \t\t\t\t\tbuckets = append(buckets, bucket)\n242: \t\t\t\t}\n243: \t\t\t}\n244: \t\t}\n245: \n246: \t\t// no distributions for rule\n247: \t\t// match is true here because it did match the segment/rule\n248: \t\tif len(validDistributions) == 0 {\n249: \t\t\ts.logger.Info(\"no distributions for rule\")\n250: \t\t\tresp.Match = true\n251: \t\t\tresp.Reason = rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\n252: \t\t\treturn resp, nil\n253: \t\t}\n254: \n255: \t\tvar (\n256: \t\t\tbucket = crc32Num(r.EntityId, r.FlagKey)\n257: \t\t\t// sort.SearchInts searches for x in a sorted slice of ints and returns the index\n258: \t\t\t// as specified by Search. The return value is the index to insert x if x is\n259: \t\t\t// not present (it could be len(a)).\n260: \t\t\tindex = sort.SearchInts(buckets, int(bucket)+1)\n261: \t\t)\n262: \n263: \t\t// if index is outside of our existing buckets then it does not match any distribution\n264: \t\tif index == len(validDistributions) {\n265: \t\t\ts.logger.Debug(\"did not match any distributions\")\n266: \t\t\tresp.Match = false\n267: \t\t\treturn resp, nil\n268: \t\t}\n269: \n270: \t\td := validDistributions[index]\n271: \t\ts.logger.Debug(\"matched distribution\", zap.Reflect(\"evaluation_distribution\", d))\n272: \n273: \t\tresp.Match = true\n274: \t\tresp.VariantKey = d.VariantKey\n275: \t\tresp.VariantAttachment = d.VariantAttachment\n276: \t\tresp.Reason = rpcevaluation.EvaluationReason_MATCH_EVALUATION_REASON\n277: \t\treturn resp, nil\n278: \t} // end rule loop\n279: \n280: \treturn resp, nil\n281: }\n282: \n283: // Boolean evaluates a request for a on the match type.\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\n559: \tconstraintMatches := 0\n560: \n561: \tvar reason string\n562: \n563: \tfor _, c := range constraints {\n564: \t\tv := evalCtx[c.Property]\n565: \n566: \t\tvar (\n567: \t\t\tmatch bool\n568: \t\t\terr   error\n569: \t\t)\n570: \n571: \t\tswitch c.Type {\n572: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_STRING_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n573: \t\t\tmatch = matchesString(c, v)\n574: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_NUMBER_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n575: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesNumber(c, v)\n576: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_BOOLEAN_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n577: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesBool(c, v)\n578: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_DATETIME_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n579: \t\t\tmatch, err = matchesDateTime(c, v)\n580: \t\tcase core.ComparisonType_ENTITY_ID_COMPARISON_TYPE:\n581: \t\t\tmatch = matchesString(c, entityId)\n582: \t\tdefault:\n583: \t\t\treturn false, reason, errs.ErrInvalid(\"unknown constraint type\")\n584: \t\t}\n585: \n586: \t\tif err != nil {\n587: \t\t\ts.logger.Debug(\"error matching constraint\", zap.String(\"property\", c.Property), zap.Error(err))\n588: \t\t\t// don't return here because we want to continue to evaluate the other constraints\n589: \t\t}\n590: \n591: \t\tif match {\n592: \t\t\t// increase the matchCount\n593: \t\t\tconstraintMatches++\n594: \n595: \t\t\tswitch segmentMatchType {\n596: \t\t\tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\n597: \t\t\t\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\n598: \t\t\t\tbreak\n599: \t\t\tdefault:\n600: \t\t\t\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\n601: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n602: \t\t\t}\n603: \t\t} else {\n604: \t\t\t// no match\n605: \t\t\tswitch segmentMatchType {\n606: \t\t\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\n607: \t\t\t\t// we can short circuit because we must match all constraints\n608: \t\t\t\tbreak\n609: \t\t\tdefault:\n610: \t\t\t\t// keep looping to see if we match the next constraint\n611: \t\t\t\tcontinue\n612: \t\t\t}\n613: \t\t}\n614: \t}\n615: \n616: \tmatched := true\n617: \n618: \tswitch segmentMatchType {\n619: \tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\n620: \t\tif len(constraints) != constraintMatches {\n621: \t\t\treason = \"did not match ALL constraints\"\n622: \t\t\tmatched = false\n623: \t\t}\n624: \n625: \tcase core.MatchType_ANY_MATCH_TYPE:\n626: \t\tif len(constraints) > 0 && constraintMatches == 0 {\n627: \t\t\treason = \"did not match ANY constraints\"\n628: \t\t\tmatched = false\n629: \t\t}\n630: \tdefault:\n631: \t\treason = fmt.Sprintf(\"unknown match type %d\", segmentMatchType)\n632: \t\tmatched = false\n633: \t}\n634: \n635: \treturn matched, reason, nil\n636: }\n637: \n638: func crc32Num(entityID string, salt string) uint {\n639: \treturn uint(crc32.ChecksumIEEE([]byte(salt+entityID))) % totalBucketNum\n640: }\n641: \n642: const (\n643: \t// totalBucketNum represents how many buckets we can use to determine the consistent hashing\n644: \t// distribution and rollout\n645: \ttotalBucketNum uint = 1000\n646: \n647: \t// percentMultiplier implies that the multiplier between percentage (100) and totalBucketNum\n648: \tpercentMultiplier float32 = float32(totalBucketNum) / 100\n649: )\n650: \n651: func matchesString(c storage.EvaluationConstraint, v string) bool {\n652: \tswitch c.Operator {\n653: \tcase flipt.OpEmpty:\n654: \t\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) == 0\n655: \tcase flipt.OpNotEmpty:\n656: \t\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) != 0\n657: \t}\n658: \n659: \tif v == \"\" {\n660: \t\treturn false\n661: \t}\n662: \n663: \tvalue := c.Value\n664: \n665: \tswitch c.Operator {\n666: \tcase flipt.OpEQ:\n667: \t\treturn value == v\n668: \tcase flipt.OpNEQ:\n669: \t\treturn value != v\n670: \tcase flipt.OpPrefix:\n671: \t\treturn strings.HasPrefix(strings.TrimSpace(v), value)\n672: \tcase flipt.OpSuffix:\n673: \t\treturn strings.HasSuffix(strings.TrimSpace(v), value)\n674: \tcase flipt.OpIsOneOf:\n675: \t\tvalues := []string{}\n676: \t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(value), &values); err != nil {\n677: \t\t\treturn false\n678: \t\t}\n679: \t\treturn slices.Contains(values, v)\n680: \tcase flipt.OpIsNotOneOf:\n681: \t\tvalues := []string{}\n682: \t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(value), &values); err != nil {\n683: \t\t\treturn false\n684: \t\t}\n685: \t\treturn !slices.Contains(values, v)\n686: \tcase flipt.OpContains:\n687: \t\treturn strings.Contains(v, value)\n688: \tcase flipt.OpNotContains:\n689: \t\treturn !strings.Contains(v, value)\n690: \t}\n691: \n692: \treturn false\n693: }\n694: \n695: func matchesNumber(c storage.EvaluationConstraint, v string) (bool, error) {\n696: \tswitch c.Operator {\n697: \tcase flipt.OpNotPresent:\n698: \t\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) == 0, nil\n699: \tcase flipt.OpPresent:\n700: \t\treturn len(strings.TrimSpace(v)) != 0, nil\n701: \t}\n702: \n703: \t// can't parse an empty string\n704: \tif v == \"\" {\n705: \t\treturn false, nil\n706: \t}\n707: \n708: \tn, err := strconv.ParseFloat(v, 64)\n709: \tif err != nil {\n710: \t\treturn false, errs.ErrInvalidf(\"parsing number from %q\", v)\n711: \t}\n712: \n713: \tswitch c.Operator {\n714: \tcase flipt.OpIsOneOf:\n715: \t\tvalues := []float64{}\n716: \t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(c.Value), &values); err != nil {\n717: \t\t\treturn false, errs.ErrInvalidf(\"Invalid value for constraint %q\", c.Value)\n718: \t\t}\n719: \t\treturn slices.Contains(values, n), nil\n720: \tcase flipt.OpIsNotOneOf:\n721: \t\tvalues := []float64{}\n722: \t\tif err := json.Unmarshal([]byte(c.Value), &values); err != nil {\n723: \t\t\treturn false, errs.ErrInvalidf(\"Invalid value for constraint %q\", c.Value)\n724: \t\t}\n725: \t\treturn !slices.Contains(values, n), nil\n726: \t}\n727: \n728: \t// TODO: we should consider parsing this at creation time since it doesn't change and it doesnt make sense to allow invalid constraint values\n729: \tvalue, err := strconv.ParseFloat(c.Value, 64)\n730: \tif err != nil {\n731: \t\treturn false, errs.ErrInvalidf(\"parsing number from %q\", c.Value)\n732: \t}\n733: \n734: matched for a segment depending\\n557: // on the match type.\\n558: func (s *Server) matchConstraints(evalCtx map[string]string, constraints []storage.EvaluationConstraint, segmentMatchType core.MatchType, entityId string) (bool, string, error) {\\n559: \\tconstraintMatches := 0\\n560: \\n561: \\tvar reason string\\n562: \\n563: \\tfor _, c := range constraints {\\n564: \\t\\tv := zap.String(\\\"property\\\", c.Property), zap.Error(err))\\n588: \\t\\t\\t// don't return here because we want to continue to evaluate the other constraints\\n589: \\t\\t}\\n590: \\n591: \\t\\tif \\t\\t\\t\\t// can short circuit here since we had at least one match\\n598: \\t\\t\\t\\tbreak\\n599: \\t\\t\\tdefault:\\n600: \\t\\t\\t\\t// keep looping as we need to match all constraints\\n601: \\t\\t\\t\\tcontinue\\n602: \\t\\t\\t}\\n603: \\t\\t} else {\\n604: \\t\\t\\t// no match\\n605: \\t\\t\\tswitch segmentMatchType {\\n606: \\t\\t\\tcase core.MatchType_ALL_MATCH_TYPE:\\n607:
